import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
import io.netty.buffer.Unpooled;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class ByteBufSlicer {
    public static List<ByteBuf> slice(ByteBuf buf, int maxSize) {
        ArrayList<ByteBuf> list = new ArrayList<ByteBuf>();
        while (buf.isReadable()) {
            list.add(buf.readSlice(Math.min(maxSize, buf.readableBytes())));
        }
        return list;
    }

    public static ByteBuf merge(List<ByteBuf> list) {
        ByteBuf buf = Unpooled.buffer();
        for (ByteBuf slice : list) {
            buf.writeBytes(slice, slice.readableBytes());
        }
        return buf;
    }
}

    public void injectSounds(Map<String, PackSounds> sounds) {
        HashMap<String, SoundRepository> map = new HashMap<String, SoundRepository>();
        for (Map.Entry<String, PackSounds> e : sounds.entrySet()) {
            if (e.getValue().repository == null) continue;
            map.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue().repository);
        }
        this.loadedSoundDomains = map;
    }

    public static enum ResourceReloadScope {
        RESOURCES(true, false),
        SOUNDS(false, true),
        ALL(true, true);

        public final boolean resources;
        public final boolean sounds;

        private ResourceReloadScope(boolean resources, boolean sounds) {
            this.resources = resources;
            this.sounds = sounds;
        }

        public static ResourceReloadScope get(boolean resources, boolean sounds) {
            return resources && sounds ? ALL : (resources ? RESOURCES : (sounds ? SOUNDS : null));
        }
    }

                }
            }
            VersionFormat.CURRENT.validate(this.name, this.version);
            if (properties.containsKey("minVersion")) {
                this.minVersion = String.valueOf(properties.get("minVersion"));
                Version.CURRENT.validate(this.name, this.minVersion);
            }
            this.onLoadProperties(properties);
        }
        catch (HeroPackMinVersionException | HeroPackVersionException e) {
            throw e;
        }
        catch (Exception e) {
            throw new HeroPackException("Reading heropack.json failed!", e);
        }
        finally {
            IOUtils.closeQuietly((Reader)reader);
        }
    }
}

    }

    private void explore(String domain, File root, File file) throws IOException {
        if (file.isDirectory()) {
            File[] files = file.listFiles();
            if (files != null) {
                for (File file1 : files) {
                    this.explore(domain, root, file1);
                }
            }
        } else if (file.isFile()) {
            String s = file.getPath().substring(root.getPath().length() + 1);
            s = s.replace(File.separatorChar, '/');
            this.storeData(new ResourceLocation(domain, s), new FileInputStream(file));
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void toBytes(ByteBuf buf) throws IOException {
        buf.writeShort(this.assets.size());
        for (Map.Entry<ResourceLocation, HandledSupplier<InputStream, IOException>> e : this.assets.entrySet()) {
            InputStream in = e.getValue().get();
            byte[] bytes = IOUtils.toByteArray((InputStream)in);
            ByteBufUtils.writeUTF8String((ByteBuf)buf, (String)e.getKey().toString());
            buf.writeInt(bytes.length);
            buf.writeBytes(bytes);
            in.close();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void fromBytes(ByteBuf buf) throws IOException {
        int size = buf.readShort();
        for (int i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
            ResourceLocation location = new ResourceLocation(ByteBufUtils.readUTF8String((ByteBuf)buf));
            this.storeData(location, buf.readBytes(buf.readInt()).array());
        }
    }

    }
